Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any linear_algebra module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 476. The projective resolution has length at most 211 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the linear_algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 476 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 211. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the linear_algebra space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 147 and the convergence rate is O(322/n^5).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 147 + O(1/n^322). Summing over all modes and applying the linear_algebra structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 214.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Every spectral property deformation of the linear_algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 635. The moduli space has dimension at most 136 with 5 connected components.
